      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n      x=.01\\linewidth,y=1cm,\n      every node/.style={font=\\footnotesize,align=center},\n      term/.style={text width=.27\\linewidth,inner sep=4pt},\n      permanent/.style={term,draw,semithick,minimum height=.95cm},\n      endpoint/.style={term,draw,thin,minimum height=.95cm},\n      excluded/.style={term,draw,dashed,thin,minimum height=.95cm},\n      differential/.style={->,thin,>=stealth}\n    ]\n    \\node[anchor=west,align=left,font=\\small] at (-14,0)\n      {\\textbf{Tensor:} level pair $(u,v)=(\\text{right},\\text{left})$;\n        total degree $t=u+v+a$.};\n    \\node at (0,-.62) {length $a=0$};\n    \\node at (34,-.62) {length $a=1$};\n    \\node at (68,-.62) {length $a=2$};\n    \\node[excluded] (tensor-sources) at (0,-1.55)\n      {$H_0(r+1,q)\\oplus H_0(r,q+1)$\\\\\n        absent at maximal $(r,q)$};\n    \\node[term] (tensor-zero) at (0,-3.15)\n      {$H_0(r,q)$\\\\$t=r+q$};\n    \\node[permanent] (tensor-middle) at (34,-3.15)\n      {$H_1(r,q)$\\\\$t=r+q+1$\\\\permanent};\n    \\node[endpoint] (tensor-two) at (68,-3.15)\n      {$H_2(r,q)=0$\\\\$t=r+q+2>q$};\n    \\draw[differential,dashed] (tensor-sources.east)\n      -- (54,-1.55) -- (tensor-two.north);\n    \\node[fill=white,inner sep=2pt] at (42,-1.55)\n      {possible incoming $d_2$};\n\n  \\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Diagram of the tensor length filtration:\n- Header labels above columns:\n  - \"Linear part: $C_0 \\xrightarrow{d_1} C_1 \\xrightarrow{d_1} C_2, \\quad H_a = H^a(C_\\bullet, d_1)$.\"\n  - \"Tensor: level pair $(u, v) = (\\text{right}, \\text{left})$; total degree $t = u + v + a$.\"\n- Three columns labeled:\n  - \"length $a = 0$\"\n  - \"length $a = 1$\"\n  - \"length $a = 2$\"\n- Nodes and boxes:\n  - Column length $a = 0$:\n    - Upper dashed box: \"$H_0(r + 1, q) \\oplus H_0(r, q + 1)$ / absent at maximal $(r, q)$\"\n    - Lower solid box: \"$H_0(r, q)$ / $t = r + q$\"\n  - Column length $a = 1$:\n    - Solid box: \"$H_1(r, q)$ / $t = r + q + 1$ / permanent\"\n  - Column length $a = 2$:\n    - Solid box: \"$H_2(r, q) = 0$ / $t = r + q + 2 > q$\"\n- Directed arrows:\n  - A dashed arrow labeled \"possible incoming $d_2$\" extends from the upper dashed box \"$H_0(r + 1, q) \\oplus H_0(r, q + 1)$\" to the solid box \"$H_2(r, q) = 0$\"."

      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n      x=.01\\linewidth,y=1cm,\n      every node/.style={font=\\footnotesize,align=center},\n      term/.style={text width=.27\\linewidth,inner sep=4pt},\n      permanent/.style={term,draw,semithick,minimum height=.95cm},\n      endpoint/.style={term,draw,thin,minimum height=.95cm},\n      excluded/.style={term,draw,dashed,thin,minimum height=.95cm},\n      differential/.style={->,thin,>=stealth}\n    ]\n    \\node[anchor=west,align=left,font=\\small] at (-14,-4.55)\n      {\\textbf{Hom:} level pair $(q,d)=(\\text{source},\\text{target})$;\n        total degree $t=d-q+a$, $d<q$.};\n    \\node at (0,-5.16) {length $a=0$};\n    \\node at (34,-5.16) {length $a=1$};\n    \\node at (68,-5.16) {length $a=2$};\n    \\node[endpoint] (hom-zero) at (0,-6.1)\n      {$H_0(M,Y^d)$, $M\\subseteq Y^q$\\\\$t=d-q<0$};\n    \\node[permanent] (hom-middle) at (34,-6.1)\n      {$H_1(M,Y^d)=0$\\\\permanent middle classes};\n    \\node[endpoint] (hom-target) at (68,-7.35)\n      {$(q,d-1)$: target block\\\\$H_2(M,Y^{d-1})$\\\\$t=d-q+1$};\n    \\node[excluded] (hom-source) at (68,-9.08)\n      {$(q+1,d)$: source-side term\\\\$f d_E^{(2)}=0$ by the cap on $I$};\n    \\draw[differential] (hom-zero.south) -- (0,-7.35)\n      -- (hom-target.west);\n    \\node[fill=white,inner sep=2pt] at (30,-7.35)\n      {$d_2$ is injective};\n    \\draw[differential,dashed] (0,-7.35) -- (0,-9.08)\n      -- (hom-source.west);\n    \\node[fill=white,text width=.38\\linewidth,inner sep=2pt] at (27,-9.08)\n      {possible source-side $d_2$\\\\\n        $Y^{q+1}_\\alpha=0$ for $\\alpha\\in\\widetilde I$};\n  \\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Diagram of the Hom length filtration:\n- Header labels above columns:\n  - \"Linear part: $C_0 \\xrightarrow{d_1} C_1 \\xrightarrow{d_1} C_2, \\quad H_a = H^a(C_\\bullet, d_1)$.\"\n  - \"Hom: level pair $(q, d) = (\\text{source}, \\text{target})$; total degree $t = d - q + a, \\quad d < q$.\"\n- Three columns labeled:\n  - \"length $a = 0$\"\n  - \"length $a = 1$\"\n  - \"length $a = 2$\"\n- Nodes and blocks:\n  - Column length $a = 0$:\n    - Solid box: \"$H_0(M, Y^d), \\quad M \\subseteq Y^q$ / $t = d - q < 0$\"\n    - A dashed vertical line extends downward to a horizontal dashed segment labeled: \"possible source-side $d_2$\" and \"$Y^{q+1}_\\alpha = 0 \\text{ for } \\alpha \\in \\widetilde{I}$\".\n  - Column length $a = 1$:\n    - Solid box: \"$H_1(M, Y^d) = 0$ / permanent middle classes\"\n  - Column length $a = 2$:\n    - Upper solid box: \"$(q, d - 1)\\text{: target block}$ / $H_2(M, Y^{d-1})$ / $t = d - q + 1$\"\n    - Lower dashed box: \"$(q + 1, d)\\text{: source-side term}$ / $f d_E^{(2)} = 0 \\text{ by the cap on } I$\"\n- Directed arrows:\n  - Solid horizontal arrow labeled \"$d_2 \\text{ is injective}$\" pointing from the column $a = 0$ vertical line to the upper solid box \"$(q, d - 1)\\text{: target block}$\".\n  - Dashed horizontal arrow pointing from the lower dashed segment under column $a = 0$ to the lower dashed box \"$(q + 1, d)\\text{: source-side term}$\"."
